     公司大事記




     January 2025
     2025 年 1 月

          January 25: Vanke Service partnered with SF Intra-city to upgrade the access solution for delivery riders.
          1月25日，萬科物業聯合順豐同城升級騎手通行解決方案。

          April 16: Vanke Service further upgraded the access solution for delivery rider in collaboration with JD
          Express and JD Takeaway. With this, second-level access to Vanke Service’s residential communities now
          covers all major delivery rider groups.
          4月16日，萬科物業聯合京東快遞、京東外賣升級騎手通行解決方案。至此，萬科物業服務小區
          秒級通行覆蓋主要騎手群體。




     February 2025
     2025 年 2 月

          February 17: Onewo launched its proprietary large model development platform, the “GC Platform”,
          marking the formal establishment of its enterprise-grade AI platform system and enabling large-scale,
          high-frequency calls.
          2月17日，萬物雲發布自研大模型開發平台GC平台，標誌着企業級AI平台體系正式成型並實現
          規模化高頻調用。




     April 2025
     2025 年 4 月

          April 25: Vanke Service, together with CRIC Property Management and the Shenzhen Property
          Management Association, released the industry’s first Research Report on Innovation and Practice of
          Smart Communities, setting a new benchmark for the property management industry.
          4 月25 日，萬科物業、克而瑞物管、深圳物協聯合發布行業首本《智慧社區創新與實踐研究報
          告》，為物業行業樹立風向標。




2   萬物雲空間科技服務股份有限公司 2025年年報
                                                                  CORPORATE MILESTONES
                                                                              公司大事記




May 2025
2025 年 5 月

    May 8: The China’s first energy audit standard, co-authored by Cushman & Wakefield Vanke Service,
    was officially implemented. This standard fills a critical gap in the field of building energy conservation,
    marking a new stage of scientific and standardized building energy management.
    5月8日，萬物梁行參編的全國首部能源審計標準正式實施，該標準填補了建築節能領域的關鍵
    空白，標誌着建築能源管理邁向科學化、標準化新階段。

    May 29: Centralcon Property Management was officially consolidated into financial statements of Vanke
    Service, further increasing the density of the Onewo Town. The “Centralcon Property Management”
    brand is retained and operates as a “Member Enterprise of Vanke Service”.
    5月29日，中洲物業正式併表萬科物業，蝶城濃度進一步提升，「中洲物業」品牌繼續保留使用，
    成為「萬科物業成員企業」。




June 2025
2025 年 6 月

    June 3: Vanke Service officially open-sourced 508 service standards and its flexible pricing system to the
    entire industry.
    6月3日，萬科物業正式向全行業開源508項服務標準及彈性定價體系。

    June 13: At the 2025 Release Conference of Comprehensive Strength Research Results of China
    Property Management Service Enterprises, Vanke Service unveiled its vision for the “Flexible Pricing
    System”: to empower property management companies and owners to jointly discuss standards
    through open sharing, creating a common industry language and co-building new service standards.
    As of December 23, 2025, Vanke Service had acquired 51 projects via this model, generating RMB165
    million in new contract value and completing contract renewals for 22 existing projects. Notably, the
    Qingdao Jinyu Lanwan project was selected as a replicable model in Shandong Province and received
    official endorsement from the Ministry of Housing and Urban-Rural Development, serving as a national
    demonstration case.
    6 月13 日，在《2025 中國物業服務企業綜合實力研究成果發布會》上萬科物業發布「彈性定價體
    系」願景：通過開放共享的方式，讓物業公司都能與業主共同討論標準，形成行業通用語言，共
    建服務新標準。截至2025年12月23日，萬科物業已通過該模式獲取51個項目、新增合同額1.65
    億元，完成 22 個既有服務項目合同換簽。其中，青島金域藍灣項目入選山東省可複製推廣經
    驗，獲住建部轉發肯定，成全國示範樣本。

     CORPORATE MILESTONES
     公司大事記




     July 2025
     2025 年 7 月

          July 5: Approved by the Shenzhen Municipal Human Resources and Social Security Bureau, Onewo
          established the “Shenzhen Postdoctoral Innovation Practice Base”. This marks a crucial step for Onewo
          in perfecting its industry-academia-research ecosystem and accelerating the commercial application of
          technological achievements.
          7月5日，萬物雲獲深圳市人力資源和社會保障局批准，設立「深圳市博士後創新實踐基地」，是
          萬物雲完善產學研鏈、加速科技成果落地應用的關鍵一環。

          July 11: Onewo established the Occupational Health and Work Safety Management Committee (HSC),
          with a subordinate Occupational Health and Work Safety Management Office (HSO), which was
          responsible for the comprehensive establishment of a work safety management system, as well as the
          prevention, management, and resolution of safety incidents.
          7月11日，萬物雲成立萬物雲職業健康與安全生產管理委員會（健安委），下設職業健康與安全生
          產管理辦公室（健安辦），負責全面安全生產管理體系搭建及安全生產事件的預防、管理與處置。




     August 2025
     2025 年 8 月

          August 25: Onewo and Winlands Fast Charge established “Yunqi Weilan”, a joint venture for charging
          pile operations. This marks Onewo’s official entry into the pan-energy sector via an equity partnership,
          setting an industry precedent for property management companies systematically operating community
          energy resources.
          8月25日，萬物雲與蔚藍快充成立充電樁運營合資公司「雲啟蔚藍」，標誌着萬物雲正式以股權合
          作模式切入泛能源賽道，開創了物業公司系統化運營社區能源資源的行業先例。

          August 25: Onewo released the Onewo Carbon Accounting Methodology and the “Carbon Pulse”
          Management Platform, becoming the first enterprise in its industry to publish independent technical
          standards for carbon accounting in China.
          8月25日，萬物雲發布《萬物雲碳核算方法論》及「碳脈搏」管理平台，成為國內首個發布獨立碳
          核算技術標準的行業企業。

                                                                               公司大事記




September 2025
2025 年 9 月

    September 1: Onewo participated for the first time and was certified as a “Top Employer China 2026”
    (the first certified enterprise in the property management industry), driving the standardized upgrade of
    its human resources management system.
    9月1日，萬物雲首次參評並獲得「2026年中國傑出僱主」認證（物業行業首家認證企業），推動人
    力管理體系標準化升級。

    September 1: Dantian Property won the bid for the Seventh Affiliated Hospital of Sun Yat-sen University,
    further deepening Onewo’s business cooperation with the university. Upon completion, the hospital will
    deploy “Lingshi”, making it the first flagship Lingshi project in the healthcare space.
    9月1日，丹田物業中標中山大學附屬第七醫院，萬物雲與中山大學的業務合作進一步加深，中
    大七院建成後將部署靈石，成為醫療空間的首個靈石標桿項目。

    September 22: Onewo released the Onewo Energy Products and Capabilities List v2.0, forming a
    systematized portfolio of energy capabilities, unifying internal terminology, and clarifying the strategic
    direction for capability building.
    9月22日，萬物雲發布《萬物雲能源產品與能力清單v2.0》，形成系統化能源能力產品圖譜，統一
    內部語言，明確戰略能力建設推進方向。

    September 27: The 23rd Vanke Service “Puli Festival” officially kicked off nationwide with over 500,000
    residents participating. During the opening ceremony, a live CPR first-aid training session successfully set
    a Guinness World Record with 20,917 concurrent online viewers. Furthermore, Vanke Service launched
    a cross-industry marketing campaign, “Eat with Meituan, Ride with DiDi, Live with Vanke Service”, in
    collaboration with top-tier companies like Meituan and DiDi.
   9月27日，第23屆萬科物業樸里節全國啟幕，超50萬居民參與。開幕式上，通過CPR急救培訓直
   播，以20,917人實時在線成功創下吉尼斯世界紀錄。萬科物業聯合美团、滴滴等頭部企業發起
   「吃上美团、行用滴滴、住在萬科物業」跨界營銷。




                                                                              Onewo Inc. Annual Report 2025        5
     CORPORATE MILESTONES
     公司大事記




     October 2025
     2025 年 10 月

          October 16: Onewo launched its Property Asset Management System, achieving dynamic full-lifecycle
          management and the integration of business and financial settlement. Acting as an asset data middle-
          office, it connects upstream and downstream systems to dynamically manage and integratedly settle
          RMB3.425 billion worth of assets. By ingesting data into a data lake, it generates various metric
          dashboards, achieving the dual goals of asset resource monitoring and facilitating asset destocking.
          10月16日，萬物雲不動產管理系統上線，實現全生命周期動態管理、業財結算一體化。作為資
          產數據中台，打通上下游系統，實現34.25億資產全生命周期動態管理及業財結算一體化。並借
          助數據入湖，輸出各類指標看板，達成資產資源監控和助力資產去化目的。

          October 25: Vanke Service, in collaboration with Tsinghua University, released the industry’s first Report
          on Carbon Neutrality Strategy and Pathway for China’s Property Management Industry. Using Vanke
          Service’s benchmark practices as a model, the report resolves industry dilemmas such as the “ambiguous
          attribution of public area emissions” and “difficulties in balancing carbon reduction costs and benefits”,
          providing a vivid community-level blueprint for achieving the “Dual Carbon” goals.
          10月25日，萬科物業聯合清華大學發布行業首份《中國物業行業碳中和戰略與路徑報告》，以萬
          科物業在標桿實踐為樣本，破解「公區排放歸屬模糊」、「減碳收益平衡難」等行業困局，為「雙
          碳」目標基層落地提供了鮮活的社區範本。




     November 2025
     2025 年 11 月

          November 26: Onewo prompted the China Property Management Association to issue an industry-wide
          initiative standardizing the handover procedures of property management projects, aimed at rectifying
          irregularities in property handovers.
          11月26日，萬物雲推動中國物業管理協會就規範物業管理項目交接行為向全行業發出倡議書，
          治理物業交接亂象。

                                                                           公司大事記




December 2025
2025 年 12 月

    December 12: Onewo’s 2nd “Rui Jian” Conference was held in Shanghai, gathering nearly 300
    representatives from renowned enterprises, media, and analysts to discuss enhancing asset returns
    through AI and low-carbon technologies. The conference announced the full-scale commercial rollout of
    “Lingshi” and pioneered a “Digital Employee” leasing model. Furthermore, Onewo jointly initiated the
    Lingshi Ecological Alliance with several prominent robotics and tech companies to drive the intelligent
    and low-carbon upgrade of property asset management.
   12月12日，萬物雲第二屆「睿見」大會在上海舉行，匯聚近300名知名企業代表以及媒體和分析
   師，共同探討借助AI與低碳技術提升資產回報。大會宣布「靈石」全面啟動商業化推廣，並首創
   「數字員工」租賃模式，聯合多家知名機器人公司、科技企業共同發起成立靈石生態聯盟，推動
   不動產管理智慧化低碳升級。

    December 24: Onewo released the OBS 1.0 system, pioneering a standardized M&A empowerment
    platform that is “calculable pre-investment and executable post-investment”. This upgrades traditional
    M&A integration from an “art” heavily reliant on personal experience to a precise “science” based on
    data and models.
    12月24日，萬物雲發布OBS1.0體系，首創「投前可計算、投後可落地」的標準化併購賦能平台，
    將傳統的併購整合從依賴個人經驗的「藝術」，升級為基於數據與模型的精準「科學」。

     “Warring States” Competition

     In 2025, the property management industry entered a critical        2025年，物業管理行業進入再定價與再分化
     stage of re-pricing and diversification. The contradiction within   的關鍵階段。行業的矛盾不再是單一的「規
     the industry is no longer solely about “scale growth”, but          模增長」，而是同時面對兩條主線：一是深
     rather involves facing two main themes simultaneously: first,       耕存量經營，應對住宅與商寫空置率抬升及
     comprehensively improving stock operations to cope with the         公共成本分攤調整帶來的利潤影響；二是重
     profit impacts caused by rising vacancy rates in residential and    構市場能力，通過資產運營、AI與機器人應
     commercial office properties and adjustments in the allocation      用及能源管理構建專業護城河。在這一背景
     of public costs; second, reconstructing market capabilities to      下，行業正加速從「經驗與人力驅動」向「治
     build a professional moat through asset operations, AI and          理與技術驅動」轉型。儘管短期衝擊顯著，
     robot applications, and energy management. Against this             但中長期的結構性機會已在分化中顯現。
     backdrop, the industry is accelerating its transformation from an
     “experience and manpower-driven” approach to a “governance
     and technology-driven” approach. Although short-term shocks
     are significant, medium-to-long-term structural opportunities
     have emerged amidst the diversification.

III.   Reshaping of Value-added Services:                           三、 多經業務重塑：從傳統的「米
       A Substantive Leap from Traditional                             面 糧 油 」向「 資 產 服 務 」實 質
       “Grain and Oil” to “Asset Services”                             性跨越

       Building New Capabilities for Value-added Services.            構建多經業務新能力。面對物業費均
       Facing the reality of falling average property management      價下跌及收繳率下滑的現實，物業公
       fees and declining collection rates, the value-added           司的多經業務正在經歷深刻的底層邏
       services of property management companies are                  輯重構。過去，多經業務高度依賴社
       undergoing a profound reconstruction of their underlying       區 流 量 ， 以「 米 面 糧 油 」等 低 門 檻 的
       logic. In the past, value-added services relied heavily on     傳統零售服務為主；如今，行業的破
       community traffic, focusing on traditional retail services     局之道在於構建資產運營能力，把握
       such as “grain and oil” with low thresholds. Today, the        「空置房」機遇，全面走向深度的「資
       path for the industry to break through lies in building        產服務」。
       asset operation capabilities, seizing the opportunity of
       “vacant units”, and comprehensively moving towards
       deep “asset services”.


       Extending Service Chain, Driving Asset Appreciation.           延伸服務鏈條，驅動資產增值。物業
       Property management companies are relying on their             企業正依託對社區物理空間的黏性，
       stickiness to community physical spaces to extend the          將服務鏈條延伸至資產的全生命週期
       service chain to the lifecycle management of assets.           管理。這其中包括但不限於：針對老
       This includes but is not limited to: renovation and repair     舊存量項目的裝修與修繕服務，提升
       services for old stock projects to enhance the residential     資產本身的居住與使用價值；針對龐
       and usage value of the assets themselves; management           大待售與空置面積的空置房管理，為
       of vacant units for the massive area awaiting sale and         產權方提供資產保育；以及在當前地
       vacant space, providing asset conservation for property        產資金鏈吃緊的大背景下，承接開發
       owners; and undertaking the destocking business of debt-       商與供應商的工抵資產去化業務，確
       offset assets from developers and suppliers against the        保自身回款健康度。通過扮演資產運
       backdrop of tight real estate capital chains, to ensure        營商的角色，物業公司正在從單純的
       the health of their own receivables. By playing the role       「社區服務者」向「資產價值捍衛者」轉
       of asset operators, property management companies              型。

     PRINCIPAL BUSINESS AND OPERATIONS                                  主要業務及經營
     The Group is a leading omni-space service provider in China. We    本集團是中國領先的全域空間服務提供商。
     are committed to upholding service forever better and enabling     我們始終堅守服務歷久彌新(service forever
     more users to experience excellent property management             better)，致力於讓更多用戶體驗物業服務之
     services. Leveraging the brand strength and professional           美好。依託於「萬科物業」、「萬物梁行」等品
     service quality of “Vanke Service (萬科物業)” and “Cushman &           牌優勢與專業服務質量，我們的業務已從基
     Wakefield Vanke Service (萬物梁行)”, our business has evolved          礎物業管理，拓展為由社區空間居住消費服
     from basic property management into an omni-space service          務、商企和城市空間綜合服務、以及AIoT及
     model driven by three synergistic systems, including community     BPaaS解決方案服務三大體系協同驅動的全
     space for residence and consumption services, commercial and       局空間服務模式。我們圍繞「資產服務、智
     enterprise and urban space for integrated services, and AIoT       慧、低碳」 三大核心，積極推進「蝶城+、企
     and BPaaS for solution services. Centered on the three core        服+、生態+」 戰略。在住宅領域，我們通過

     competitive advantages through technological and business              於：科技研發、機器人採購與部署、蝶城改
     model innovations, while offsetting industry-wide cost                 造等前期資本性支出與費用將在當期或通過
     pressures. However, influencing factors include, but are not           折舊攤銷影響利潤；
     limited to: upfront capital expenditures and expenses related to
     technological R&D, the procurement and deployment of robotics,
     and Onewo Town renovations, which will weigh on profitability
     either in the current period or subsequently through depreciation
     and amortization;

      DIVIDEND POLICY                                                    股息政策
      The declaration and payment of dividends shall be determined       宣派及派付股息將由董事會全權酌情釐定。
      at the sole discretion of the Board. The Board shall also take     於考慮是否建議派付股息及釐定股息金額
      into account the following factors when considering whether        時，董事會亦應考慮下列因素：我們的經營
      to propose dividends and determining the dividend amount:          業績、現金流量、財務狀況、附屬公司向我
      our results of operations, cash flows, financial condition,        們派付的現金股息、業務前景、有關我們宣
      payments by our subsidiaries of cash dividends to us, business     派及派付股息的法定、監管及合約限制、總
      prospects, statutory, regulatory and contractual restrictions on   體經濟狀況、本集團業務的業務週期及可能
      our declaration and payment of dividends, general economic         對本集團業務或財務表現及狀況產生影響的
      conditions, business cycle of the Group’s business and other       其他內部或外部因素，以及董事會可能認為

      well as other factors that our Board may consider important. No

of development strategies. The Group’s internal control is a          死循環管理督辦機制，準確歸因到各級責任

      判斷及估計包括採納適當的模型及方法進行商譽減值評                                       家參與的情況下，評估管理層就商譽減值評估所採納
      估，以及在使用價值計算中採用重要假設，有關假設主                                       的模型及方法和管理層所採用的關鍵假設；
      要包括年收入增長率、長期增長率及稅前貼現率。
                                                                 •   通過比較過往年度的現金流量預測與本年度的實際現
      鑒於商譽結餘乃屬重大且管理層在進行商譽減值評估時                                       金流量來進行追溯性審查，以評估管理層預測過程的
      作出重大判斷及估計，我們認為此領域為一項關鍵審計                                       可靠性及歷史準確性；
      事項。

      會計政策及披露載於合併財務報表附註3及附註17。

      STATEMENTS

      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標是對合併財務報表整體是否不存

      guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             《香港審計準則》進行的審計總能發現存在某
      will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             一重大錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯
      Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         誤引起，如果合理預期其單獨或匯總起來可

      be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken         表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可
      on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               被視作重大。

      throughout the audit. We also:                                         度。我們亦：

             to fraud or error, design and perform audit procedures              險，設計及執行審計程序以應對這些

             resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from          假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因

      1.      CORPORATE AND GROUP                                        1.   公司及集團資料
              INFORMATION

              Onewo Inc. (the “Company”) was incorporated in the               萬物雲空間科技服務股份有限公司
              People’s Republic of China (“PRC”) on 20 February 2001          （「本公司」）於2001年2月20日在中華
              as a limited liability company. On 20 March 2018, the            人民共和國（「中國」）註冊成立為一家
              Company was converted into a joint stock company with            有限公司。於2018年3月20日，本公
              limited liability under the Company Law of the PRC.              司根據《中華人民共和國公司法》改制
                                                                               為股份有限公司。

              During the year, the Group was involved in the following        年內，本集團從事以下主營業務：
              principal activities:


              •      Community space living consumption services              •   社區空間居住消費服務
              •      Commercial and urban space integrated services           •   商企和城市空間綜合服務
              •      AIoT and BPaaS solution services                         •   AIoT及BPaaS解決方案服務

              In the opinion of the directors, the ultimate holding           董事認為，本公司的最終控股公司
              company of the Company is China Vanke Co., Ltd. (the            為 萬 科 企 業 股 份 有 限 公 司（「 萬 科 企
              “China Vanke”), a public company established in the PRC         業」，一家於中國成立的上市公司，其
              with its shares listed on Shenzhen Stock Exchange and           股份在深圳證券交易所和香港聯合交
              The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock             易所有限公司（「聯交所」）上市）。
              Exchange”).

1.   CORPORATE AND GROUP                                                 1.      公司及集團資料（續）

     Information about subsidiaries                                              有關附屬公司資料

                     shall assess whether a currency is exchangeable                本）訂明實體應如何評估某種貨

                     exchangeability is lacking. The amendments require             下，實體應如何估計計量日期
                     disclosures of information that enable users of                的即期匯率。該等修訂要求披
                     financial statements to understand the impact                  露使財務報表使用者能夠了解

      2.      ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                               2.   會計政策（續）
              2.5 Material accounting policies                                   2.5 重要會計政策

                     Investments in associates and joint ventures                  於聯營公司及合營企業之投資

                     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a            聯營公司是指本集團於其擁有
                     long-term interest of generally not less than 20%             長期權益（通常不低於股權表決
                     of the equity voting rights and over which it has             權的20%），並對其有重大影響
                     significant influence. Significant influence is the           的主體。重大影響力指有權參
                     power to participate in the financial and operating           與投資對象的財務及營運政策
                     policy decisions of the investee, but is not control          決策，但對該等決策並無控制
                     or joint control over those policies.                         權或共同控制權。

                     A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement                合營企業是指共同控制一項安
                     whereby the parties that have joint control of the            排的參與方對該合營企業的淨
                     arrangement have rights to the net assets of the              資產享有權利的合營安排。共
                     joint venture. Joint control is the contractually             同控制是指按合同約定分享對
                     agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                  一項安排的控制權，並且在相
                     which exists only when decisions about the relevant           關活動的決策中要求分享控制
                     activities require the unanimous consent of the               權的參與方一致同意時才存在。
                     parties sharing control.


                     The Group’s investments in associates and joint               本集團於聯營公司及合營企業
                     ventures are stated in the consolidated statement             之投資在合併財務狀況表中
                     of financial position at the Group’s share of net             以權益會計法按本集團應佔資
                     assets under the equity method of accounting, less            產淨值扣除任何減值虧損後列
                     any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to                示。已作出調整以使任何可能
                     bring into line any dissimilar accounting policies            存在的不同會計政策與此一致。
                     that may exist.

                     Business combinations and goodwill                               業務合併及商譽

                     Business combinations are accounted for using the                業務合併乃以收購法入賬。轉
                     acquisition method. The consideration transferred                讓對價乃以收購日期的公允價
                     is measured at the acquisition date fair value which             值計量，該公允價值為本集團
                     is the sum of the acquisition date fair values of                轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允
                     assets transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed             價值、本集團對被收購方前擁
                     by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree                有人承擔的負債及本集團發行
                     and the equity interests issued by the Group in                  以換取被收購方控制權的股本
                     exchange for control of the acquiree. For each                   權益的總和。就各業務合併而
                     business combination, the Group elects whether                   言，本集團選擇是否以公允價
                     to measure the non-controlling interests in the                  值或應佔被收購方可識別資產
                     acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate                   淨值的比例，計量於被收購方
                     share of the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All             的非控股權益。非控股權益的
                     other components of non-controlling interests are                所有其他組成部分均按公允價
                     measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are            值計量。收購相關成本於產生
                     expensed as incurred.                                            時支銷。

                     The Group determines that it has acquired a                      本集團認為，當所收購的一組
                     business when the acquired set of activities and                 活動及資產包括一項投入及一
                     assets includes an input and a substantive process               項實質過程，而兩者對創造產
                     that together significantly contribute to the ability            出的能力有重大貢獻，其已收
                     to create outputs.                                               購一項業務。

          Business combinations and goodwill (continued)                   業務合併及商譽（續）

          When the Group acquires a business, it assesses                  當本集團收購業務時，須根據
          the financial assets and liabilities assumed for                 合約條款、收購日期的經濟環
          appropriate classification and designation in                    境及相關條件對所承擔的金融
          accordance with the contractual terms, economic                  資產及負債進行評估，以進行
          circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the                 適當分類及指定，包括將被收
          acquisition date. This includes the separation of                購方主合約中的嵌入式衍生工
          embedded derivatives in host contracts of the                    具分開。
          acquiree.


          If the business combination is achieved in stages,               如果業務合併分階段進行，則
          the previously held equity interest is remeasured                先前所持股本權益按其收購日
          at its acquisition date fair value and any resulting             期公允價值重新計量，而就此
          gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss or other            產生的任何收益或虧損於損益

          acquisition date. Contingent consideration classified            分類為資產或負債的或有對價
          as an asset or liability is measured at fair value with          按公允價值計量，而公允價值
          changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss.              的變動於損益確認。分類為權
          Contingent consideration that is classified as equity            益的或有對價不予重新計量，

                     Fair value measurement                                           公允價值計量

                     The Group measures its equity investments                        本集團於各報告期末按公允價
                     designated at fair value through other                           值計量其指定為以公允價值計
                     comprehensive income and financial assets at fair                量且其變動計入其他全面收益
                     value through profit or loss at the end of each                  的股權投資及以公允價值計量
                     reporting period. Fair value is the price that would             且其變動計入當期損益的金融
                     be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a               資產。公允價值是指市場參與
                     liability in an orderly transaction between market               者在計量日的有序交易中，出
                     participants at the measurement date. The fair                   售資產所收取或轉讓負債所支
                     value measurement is based on the presumption                    付的價格。公允價值計量乃基
                     that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer               於假設出售資產或轉讓負債的
                     the liability takes place either in the principal                交易於資產或負債的主要市場
                     market for the asset or liability, or in the absence             或（在無主要市場情況下）於資
                     of a principal market, in the most advantageous                  產或負債的最有利市場進行。
                     market for the asset or liability. The principal or the          主要或最有利市場應當是本集
                     most advantageous market must be accessible by                   團能夠進入的市場。資產或負
                     the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability             債的公允價值乃基於市場參與
                     is measured using the assumptions that market                    者為資產或負債定價時所用之
                     participants would use when pricing the asset or                 假設計量（假設市場參與者依照
                     liability, assuming that market participants act in              其最佳經濟利益行事）。
                     their economic best interest.


                     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset                非金融資產的公允價值計量須
                     takes into account a market participant’s ability to             計及市場參與者通過使用該資
                     generate economic benefits by using the asset in                 產的最高及最佳用途，或將該
                     its highest and best use or by selling it to another             資產出售予將使用其最高及最
                     market participant that would use the asset in its               佳用途的另一市場參與者而產
                     highest and best use.                                            生經濟利益的能力。

                     component or for which the Group has applied                      易應收款項及應收保證金外，
                     the practical expedient of not adjusting the effect               本集團初步以其公允價值加（對
                     of a significant financing component, the Group                   於並非以公允價值計量且其變
                     initially measures a financial asset at its fair value,           動計入當期損益的金融資產）交
                     plus in the case of a financial asset not at fair value           易成本計量金融資產。不包含
                     through profit or loss, transaction costs. Trade                  重大融資成分或本集團已應用

                     “Revenue recognition” below.


                     In order for a financial asset to be classified and               為使金融資產按攤銷成本或以
                     measured at amortised cost or fair value through                  公允價值計量且其變動計入其

                     to cash flows that are solely payments of principal               金融資產需要產生純粹為支付

                     outstanding. Financial assets with cash flows that                粹為支付本金及利息」）的現金
                     are not SPPI are classified and measured at fair                  流量。就現金流量並非純粹為
                     value through profit or loss, irrespective of the                 支付本金及利息之金融資產而
                     business model.                                                   言，不論其業務模式如何，均

                     exposures for which there has not been a                        認。就自初始確認以來信貸風

                     that result from default events that are possible               來 12 個月內可能發生的違約事

                     those credit exposures for which there has been                 個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始
                     a significant increase in credit risk since initial             確認以來信貸風險顯著增加的
                     recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit            信貸風險項目而言，須於風險
                     losses expected over the remaining life of the                  剩餘年期內就預期信貸虧損計

                     lifetime ECL).                                                  發生（存續期預期信貸虧損）。

                     At each reporting date, the Group assesses                      於各報告日期，本集團會評估
                     whether the credit risk on a financial instrument               金融工具的信貸風險自初始確
                     has increased significantly since initial recognition.          認以來是否顯著增加。進行評
                     When making the assessment, the Group compares                  估時，本集團比較金融工具於

                     instrument as at the reporting date with the risk               金融工具於初始確認日期出現
                     of a default occurring on the financial instrument              違約之風險，並考慮合理及有
                     as at the date of initial recognition and considers             理據且毋須花費不必要成本或
                     reasonable and supportable information that is                  精力即可獲得之資料，包括過
                     available without undue cost or effort, including               往及前瞻性資料。本集團認
                     historical and forward-looking information. The                 為，當合約付款逾期逾30 天
                     Group considers that there has been a significant               時，信貸風險會顯著增加。
                     increase in credit risk when contractual payments

              2.5 Material accounting policies (continued)                           2.5 重要會計政策（續）

                     Financial liabilities (continued)                                 金融負債（續）

                     Financial liabilities at amortised cost                           按攤銷成本計量的金融負債

                     After initial recognition, trade and notes payables,              於初始確認後，貿易應付款項
                     other payables and accruals, interest-bearing bank                及應付票據、其他應付款項及
                     borrowings and lease liabilities are subsequently                 應計項目、計息銀行借款及租
                     measured at amortised cost, using the effective                   賃負債採用實際利率法，按攤
                     interest rate method unless the effect of                         銷成本進行後續計量，但貼現
                     discounting would be immaterial, in which case                    的影響屬微不足道則除外，而
                     they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are                     在該情況下將按成本列賬。當
                     recognised in the statement of profit or loss when                負債終止確認且按實際利率攤
                     the liabilities are derecognised as well as through               銷過程計算時，收益及虧損於
                     the effective interest rate amortisation process.                 損益表中確認。

                     Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account               攤銷成本於計及收購時的任何
                     any discount or premium on acquisition and fees                   貼現或溢價及為實際利率組成
                     or costs that are an integral part of the effective               部分的費用或成本後計算。實
                     interest rate. The effective interest rate amortisation           際利率攤銷計入損益表中的財
                     is included in finance costs in the statement of                  務成本。
                     profit or loss.


                     Derecognition of financial liabilities                            終止確認金融負債

                     A financial liability is derecognised when the                    當負債項下的責任已解除或取
                     obligation under the liability is discharged or                   消或屆滿時，即會終止確認金
                     cancelled, or expires.                                            融負債。

                     When an existing financial liability is replaced by               如果現有金融負債被另一項由
                     another from the same lender on substantially                     同一放貸人按差異較大的條款
                     different terms, or the terms of an existing liability            或按現有負債經大幅修改後的
                     are substantially modified, such an exchange or                   條款提供的債項所取代，則有
                     modification is treated as a derecognition of the                 關更換或修改被視為終止確認
                     original liability and a recognition of a new liability,          原有負債及確認新負債，而其
                     and the difference between the respective carrying                各自賬面值的差額於損益表中
                     amounts is recognised in the statement of profit or               確認。
                     loss.

     when the carrying value of an asset or a cash-generating               額（即其公允價值減出售成本與其使

     its fair value less costs of disposal and its value-in-use. The        值。公允價值減出售成本的計算乃基

     Leases — Estimating the incremental                                    租賃 － 估計增量借款利率
     borrowing rate

     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate                   本集團無法隨時釐定租賃中隱含的利
     implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental             率，因此，其使用增量借款利率計量
     borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR           租賃負債。增量借款利率為本集團於
     is the rate of interest that the Group would have to pay to            類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權資
     borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security, the           產價值相近的資產，而以類似抵押品
     funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to               於類似期間借入所需資金應支付的利
     the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.              率。因此，增量借款利率反映了本集
     The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have                  團「應支付」的利率，當無可觀察的利
     to pay”, which requires estimation when no observable                  率時（例如就未訂立融資交易的附屬
     rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not              公司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以
     enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to be              反映租賃的條款及條件時，則須作出
     adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease.             估計。

      4.      OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION                                          4.   經營分部資料
              The Group identifies operating segments and prepares                        本集團根據向執行董事報告的定期內
              segment information based on the regular internal                           部財務資料確定經營分部並編製分部
              financial information reported to the executive directors                   資料，供執行董事決定對本集團業務
              for their decisions about resources allocation to the                       組成部分的資源分配及審查該等組成
              Group’s business components and for their review of                         部分的業績。向執行董事報告的內部
              the performance of those components. The business                           財務資料中的業務組成部分乃按照本
              components in the internal financial information reported                   集團的主要產品線釐定。
              to the executive directors are determined following the
              Group’s major product lines.


              During the reporting period, the Group was principally                      於報告期內，本集團主要在中國提供
              engaged in the provision of community space living                          社區空間居住消費服務、商企和城市
              consumption services, commercial and urban space                            空間綜合服務以及AIoT及BPaaS解決
              integrated services and AIoT and BPaaS solution services in                 方案服務。為決定資源分配，管理層
              the PRC. Management reviews the operating results of the                    將業務的經營業績作為單一經營分部
              business as a single operating segment to make decisions                    來審查。因此，執行董事認為，僅一
              about resources to be allocated. Therefore, the executive                   個分部用於作出戰略決策。
              directors regard that there is only one segment which is

           The Group has elected to file a combined tax return                本集團已選擇提交合併報稅表，併入
           incorporating assessable profit and tax losses attributable        本集團應佔以及本集團按管理酬金制
           to the Group as well as certain properties which are               管理的若干物業的應課稅利潤及稅項
           managed by the Group on management remuneration                    虧損。由於此安排，本集團可暫時動
           basis. As a result of such an arrangement, the Group               用管理物業的稅項虧損，從而推遲支
           is able to temporarily utilise tax losses from managing            付若干撥備。動用該等管理物業的稅
           properties, resulting in the deferral of payment of                項虧損及本集團推遲支付企業所得稅
           certain provisions. The utilisation of such tax losses             撥備對報告期間的合併損益表並無影
           from managing properties and the deferral of the                   響。就財務會計而言，本集團已按其
           Group’s payment of EIT provisions had no effect on                 物業管理附屬公司的適用稅率，基於
           the consolidated statement of profit or loss during the            應課稅利潤借記所得稅開支並貸記稅
           reporting period. For financial accounting purposes,               項負債，以便作出相關撥備。
           the Group has made relevant provision by debiting the
           income tax expense and crediting tax liabilities based on
           assessable profits at the applicable tax rates of its property
           management subsidiaries.

    The recoverable amounts of the CGUs are determined             現金產生單位的可收回金額乃根據獲
    based on value-in-use of the CGUs to which the goodwill        分配商譽的現金產生單位的使用價值
    is allocated. These calculations use pre-tax cash flow         釐定。該等計算使用基於管理層批准
    projections based on financial budgets of the CGUs             的五年期現金產生單位財務預算的稅
    approved by management covering a five-year period.            前現金流量預測。超過五年期的現金
    Cash flows beyond the five-year period are extrapolated        流量使用以下估計長期增長率進行推
    using the estimated long-term growth rates below. The          算。該增長率未超過現金產生單位經
    growth rates do not exceed the long-term average growth        營所在相關行業的長期平均增長率。
    rate for the related industry in which the CGUs operate.       以下稅前貼現率反映了與相關行業及
    The pre-tax discount rates below reflect specific risks        現金產生單位本身相關的特定風險以
    relating to the relevant industry and the CGUs themselves      及相關地區的宏觀環境。
    and macro-environment of the relevant region.


    The key assumptions used in the estimation of value-in-use     使用價值估算所用主要假設如下：
    were as follows:


                                                                                2025             2024
                                                                              2025年            2024年


    Annual growth rates of revenue             收入年增長率                        7%~9%         10%~11%
    Long-term growth rate                      長期增長率                              2%              2%
    Pre-tax discount rates                     稅前貼現率                       16%~17%         16%~17%


    The Group assessed the impairment of goodwill at the end       本集團於報告期末對商譽進行減值評
    of the reporting period and the recoverable amounts of         估，各現金產生單位的可收回金額超
    each CGUs had exceeded each of their carrying amounts,         過其各自的賬面價值，因此商譽不進
    and hence the goodwill was not regarded as impaired.           行減值。

      30. RESERVES                                                       30. 儲備
          The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements            於當前及過往年度，本集團的儲備及
          therein for the current and prior years are presented in         其變動金額呈列於財務報表第 173 至
          the consolidated statement of changes in equity on pages         174頁的合併權益變動表。
          173 to 174 of the financial statements.


          (i)     Capital reserve                                          (i)     資本儲備

                  The capital reserve of the Group represents 1) the               本集團的資本儲備是指：1)已
                  difference between the par value of the shares                   發行股份面值與收到的對價
                  issued and the consideration received; 2) the excess             之間的差額；2)對價超過已收
                  of the consideration over the carrying amount of                 購 ╱ 出售的非控股權益的賬面
                  the non-controlling interests acquired/disposed;                 值部分；及 3）已授出的以股份
                  and 3) the fair value of the share-base payment                  為基礎的付款的公允價值。
                  granted.


          (ii)    Fair value reserve of financial assets at fair value     (ii)    以公允價值計量且其變動計入
                  through other comprehensive income                               其他全面收益的金融資產的公
                                                                                   允價值儲備

                  The fair value reserve of financial assets at fair               以公允價值計量且其變動計入
                  value through other comprehensive income                         其他全面收益的金融資產的公
                  comprises all revaluation changes arising from the               允價值儲備包括指定為以公允
                  equity instruments designated at fair value through              價值計量且其變動計入其他全
                  other comprehensive income.                                      面收益的權益工具產生的所有
                                                                                   重估變動。

          (iii)   Exchange fluctuation reserve                             (iii)   匯率波動儲備

                  The exchange fluctuation reserve represents                      匯率波動儲備是指功能貨幣與
                  exchange differences arising from the translation                本集團列報貨幣不同的海外業
                  of the financial statements of foreign operations                務的財務報表折算所產生的匯
                  whose functional currencies are different from the               率差異。
                  Group’s presentation currency.

           with PRC GAAP, to the statutory surplus reserve                  直至該公積金達到註冊資本的
           until the reserve reaches 50% of the registered                  50%。向該公積金的轉賬必須
           capital. The transfer to this reserve must be                    在向股東分派股息之前進行。
           made before the distribution of a dividend to
           shareholders.

    that in the event of liquidation and, subject to certain         進行清算，在中國相關法規規定的若

35. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE                                    35. 金融工具的公允價值及公
   HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                            允價值層級（續）
   INSTRUMENTS (continued)

   The Group’s finance department headed by the finance            本集團的財務部門由財務經理領導，
   manager is responsible for determining the policies and         負責確定金融工具公允價值計量的政
   procedures for the fair value measurement of financial          策和程序。財務部門直接向首席財務
   instruments. The finance department reports directly to         官匯報。財務部門於各報告日期分析
   the chief financial officer. At each reporting date, the        金融工具的價值變動，並確定估值中
   finance department analyses the movements in the values         應用的主要輸入數據。估值由首席財
   of financial instruments and determines the major inputs        務官審閱及批准。
   applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and
   approved by the chief financial officer.


   The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are     金融資產及負債的公允價值乃按該
   included at the amount at which the instrument could            工具於自願交易雙方當前交易（而非
   be exchanged in a current transaction between willing           強迫或清盤銷售）下的可交易金額入
   parties, other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The        賬。用於估計公允價值的方法及假設
   following methods and assumptions were used to estimate         如下：
   the fair values:


   The fair values of the non-current portion of financial         計入預付款項、按金及其他應收款項
   assets included in prepayments, deposits and other              的金融資產的非即期部分的公允價
   receivables, the non-current portion of other payables          值、其他應付款項及應計項目的非即
   and accruals and lease liabilities have been calculated by      期部分的公允價值以及租賃負債的公
   discounting the expected future cash flows using rates          允價值乃通過採用具有類似條款、信
   currently available for instruments with similar terms,         貸風險及剩餘期限的工具目前可用的
   credit risk and remaining maturities. The changes in fair       利率，按預期未來現金流量進行貼現

          OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES

          The Group’s principal financial instruments mainly include            本集團的主要金融工具主要包括受限
          restricted bank deposits, cash and cash equivalents, trade            制銀行存款、現金及現金等價物、貿
          and other receivables, trade and other payables, which                易及其他應收款項、貿易及其他應付
          arise directly from its operations. The Group has other               款項，均直接來自其經營業務。本集
          financial assets and liabilities such as financial assets at fair     團擁有其他金融資產及負債，如以公
          value through profit or loss, time deposits with original             允價值計量且其變動計入當期損益的
          maturity of over three months, lease liabilities, amounts             金融資產、原到期日超過三個月的定
          due to related parties and amounts due from related                   期存款、租賃負債、應付關聯方款項
          parties. The main purpose of these financial instruments is           及應收關聯方款項。該等金融工具的
          to raise finance for the Group’s operations.                          主要目的是為本集團的營運籌集資金。

          The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                     本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外
          instruments are foreign currency risk, credit risk and                匯風險、信貸風險及流動性風險。董
          liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews and agrees             事會檢討及議定政策，以管理各項風
          policies for managing each of these risks and they are                險，概述如下。
          summarised below.


          Foreign currency risk                                                 外匯風險

          Foreign currency risk is the risk of loss resulting from              外匯風險是指因外幣匯率變動而造成
          changes in foreign exchange rates. Fluctuations in                    損失的風險。人民幣與本集團經營業
          exchange rates between RMB and other currencies                       務所用其他貨幣之間的匯率波動可能
          in which the Group conducts business may affect the                   會影響本集團的財務狀況及經營業
          Group’s financial condition and results of operations. As at          績。於報告期末，由於本集團並未持
          the end of the reporting period, since the Group did not              有任何以各經營單位功能貨幣以外的
          hold any financial instruments denominated in currencies              貨幣計值的金融工具，故本集團並未
          other than the functional currencies of the respective                面臨任何重大外匯風險。
          operating units, the Group did not have any significant
          exposure to the foreign currency risk.

   counterparties.

   basis.                                                           項結餘。

   Capital management                                              資本管理

   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management        本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本
   are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going     集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健的資
   concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to      本比率，以支持其業務並將股東價值
   support its business and maximise shareholders’ value.          最大化。

   The Group manages its capital structure and makes               本集團根據經濟狀況變化及相關資產
   adjustments to it in light of changes in economic               的風險特徵來管理及調整其資本架
   conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying       構。為維持或調整資本架構，本集團
   assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the        可能會調整向股東派發的股息、向股
   Group may adjust the dividend payment to shareholders,          東返還資本或發行新股。
   return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.


   The Group monitors capital on the basis of the net gearing      本集團以淨負債率作為監控資本的基
   ratio. This ratio is calculated as total interest-bearing       準。淨負債率為按計息借款總額減去
   borrowings minus cash and cash equivalents divided by           現金及現金等價物除以截至報告期末
   total equity as of the end of the reporting period.             的權益總額計算。

   As at 31 December 2025 and 2024, the Group maintained           於2025年及2024 年12 月31 日，本集
   at net cash position.                                           團保持淨現金狀況。
